# ROMS biology(생태계) 프레임워크 — 문서와 코드 차이 (10-14 대조)

출처: [biology](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-biology-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론), [outputs-metadata](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-outputs-metadata-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론), [sediment-ice-vegetation](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-sediment-ice-vegetation-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론), [coupling-nesting-parallel](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-coupling-nesting-parallel-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론), [inputs](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-inputs-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론).
각 보고서 끝의 전수 검증 기록 (10-14)을 최종 결론으로 따른다.

문서 판: ROMS 위키 wikitext, 2026-05-03 받음, 쪽별 revid는 [manifest.json](../raw/manuals/wiki/manifest.json). 코드 판: 원본 저장소 HEAD `57aecf58`, 2026-09-18, roms-4.2 이후 4.3 개발.

식물플랑크톤과 작은 detritus를 각각 wPhy와 wDet로 침강시킨다. LINEAR_CONTINUATION, NEUMANN, 기본 경계 재구성을 구분한다. |
| biology:CF-048 | npzd_Powell — Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes to tracer array in time index "nnew". Index "nnew" is solution after advection and mixing and has transport units (m Tunits) hence the increment is multiplied by Hz.  Notice that we need to subtract original values "Bio_old" at the top of the routine to just account for the concentractions affected by BGC processes. This also takes into account any constraints (non-negative concentrations, carbon concentration range) specified before entering BGC kernel. If "Bio" were unchanged by BGC processes, the increment would be exactly zero. Notice that final tracer values, t(:,:,:,nnew,:) are not bounded >=0 so that we can preserve total inventory of nutrients when advection causes tracer concentration to go negative. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:634` `!  Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:649` `          ibio=idbio(itrc)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:652` `              cff=Bio(i,k,ibio)-Bio_old(i,k,ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:653` `              t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)=t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)+cff*Hz(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:123` `# elif defined NPZD_POWELL`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:124` `#  include <npzd_Powell.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NPZD_POWELL은 내부 Bio와 Bio_old의 차이에 Hz를 곱해 nnew tracer를 갱신한다. 최종 tracer의 비음수 제한은 다시 적용하지 않는다. |
| biology:CF-049 | npzd_iron 내부 수치 상수 | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:49` `      character (len=*), parameter :: MyFile =                          &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:50` `     &  __FILE__`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:147` `      integer, parameter :: Nsink = 2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:153` `      real(r8), parameter :: MinVal = 1.0e-6_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:121` `# elif defined NPZD_IRON`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:122` `#  include <npzd_iron.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NPZD_IRON은 Nsink=2와 MinVal=1.0e-6_r8을 선언한다. |

| biology:CF-053 | npzd_iron — Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes to tracer array in time index "nnew". Index "nnew" is solution after advection and mixing and has transport units (m Tunits) hence the increment is multiplied by Hz.  Notice that we need to subtract original values "Bio_old" at the top of the routine to just account for the concentractions affected by BGC processes. This also takes into account any constraints (non-negative concentrations, carbon concentration range) specified before entering BGC kernel. If "Bio" were unchanged by BGC processes, the increment would be exactly zero. Notice that final tracer values, t(:,:,:,nnew,:) are not bounded >=0 so that we can preserve total inventory of nutrients when advection causes tracer concentration to go negative. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:758` `!  Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:773` `          ibio=idbio(itrc)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:776` `              cff=Bio(i,k,ibio)-Bio_old(i,k,ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:777` `              t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)=t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)+cff*Hz(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:121` `# elif defined NPZD_IRON`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:122` `#  include <npzd_iron.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NPZD_IRON은 Bio와 Bio_old의 차이에 Hz를 곱해 nnew tracer를 갱신한다. 최종 tracer의 비음수 제한은 다시 적용하지 않는다. |

| biology:CF-056 | nemuro — Start internal iterations to achieve convergence of the nonlinear backward-implicit solution. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:271` `! day time`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:342` `                dz=0.5_r8*(z_w(i,j,k)-z_w(i,j,k-1))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:343` `                kappaS=AttSW(ng)+                                       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:344` `     &                 AttPS(ng)*(Bio(i,k,iSphy)+Bio(i,k,iLphy))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:345` `                kappaL=AttSW(ng)+                                       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:346` `     &                 AttPL(ng)*(Bio(i,k,iSphy)+Bio(i,k,iLphy))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:347` `                IrrS=EXP(-kappaS*dz)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:348` `                IrrL=EXP(-kappaL*dz)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:349` `                AttS=AttS*IrrS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:350` `                AttL=AttL*IrrL`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:351` `                LightS(i,k)=(1.0_r8-EXP(-alphaPS(ng)*AttS*cff1))*       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:352` `     &                      EXP(-betaPS(ng)*AttS*cff1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:353` `                LightL(i,k)=(1.0_r8-EXP(-alphaPL(ng)*AttL*cff2))*       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:354` `     &                      EXP(-betaPL(ng)*AttL*cff2)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:358` `                AttS=AttS*IrrS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:359` `                AttL=AttL*IrrL`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:363` `                LightS(i,k)=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:364` `                LightL(i,k)=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:117` `# elif defined NEMURO`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:118` `#  include <nemuro.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NEMURO는 BioIter 횟수만큼 내부 반응을 반복한다. 양의 표면 광량에서는 두 식물플랑크톤의 광 제한을 계산하고 그 외에는 LightS와 LightL을 0으로 둔다. |

IVLEV_IMPLICIT 분기도 있으나 SOLVE3D의 checkdefs는 앞의 두 옵션이 모두 없는 구성을 거부한다. 두 허용 옵션을 동시에 켠 구성도 거부한다. |

BIO_SEDIMENT이면 저층 유출을 각각 NO3와 SiOH로 돌려준다. |
| biology:CF-063 | nemuro — Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes to tracer array in time index "nnew". Index "nnew" is solution after advection and mixing and has transport units (m Tunits) hence the increment is multiplied by Hz.  Notice that we need to subtract original values "Bio_old" at the top of the routine to just account for the concentractions affected by BGC processes. This also takes into account any constraints (non-negative concentrations, carbon concentration range) specified before entering BGC kernel. If "Bio" were unchanged by BGC processes, the increment would be exactly zero. Notice that final tracer values, t(:,:,:,nnew,:) are not bounded >=0 so that we can preserve total inventory of nutrients when advection causes tracer concentration to go negative. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:1030` `!  Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:1045` `          ibio=idbio(itrc)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:1048` `              cff=Bio(i,k,ibio)-Bio_old(i,k,ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/nemuro.h:1049` `              t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)=t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)+cff*Hz(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:117` `# elif defined NEMURO`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:118` `#  include <nemuro.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NEMURO는 Bio와 Bio_old의 차이에 Hz를 곱해 nnew tracer를 갱신한다. 최종 tracer의 비음수 제한은 다시 적용하지 않는다. |
| biology:CF-064 | ecosim 내부 수치 상수 | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:75` `      character (len=*), parameter :: MyFile =                          &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:76` `     &  __FILE__`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:194` `      integer, parameter :: Msink = 30`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:204` `      real(r8), parameter :: MinVal = 0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:113` `# elif defined ECOSIM`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:114` `#  include <ecosim.h>` | C 문서 누락 | ECOSIM은 침강 작업 배열 한계 Msink=30과 내부 농도 하한 MinVal=0.0_r8을 선언한다. |

ECOSIM은 세균 원소별 Bio_new를 자기 자신만큼 빼서 먼저 0으로 만든다. 뒤의 fecal·DOM·무기물 분배식은 그 0이 된 값을 다시 사용한다. BioIter가 양수이고 유한한 양의 세균 성장 증가량이 있어도 이 블록의 분배량은 0이 되는 E 강함 후보이다. |

Regen_flag     Logical switch (T/F) to calculate fecal matter regeneration.` | E 코드 결함 후보 (강함) | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECOSIM이 선택될 때의 계산이다. ECOSIM은 Regen_flag가 참일 때만 지역 Regen_C·Regen_N·Regen_S·Regen_P·Regen_F 배열을 대입한다. 뒤의 fecal 재광물화 루프에는 같은 가드가 없다. BioIter가 양수이고 Regen_flag=F이면 미대입 지역 배열을 읽는 E 강함 후보이다. 입력 후처리의 RegCR 등 매개변수 0 대입은 이 지역 배열을 초기화하지 않는다. |

또는 C:N 조건으로 색소 목표 비율을 정한다. 유효 색소 인덱스와 양의 농도 조건에서 색소 증가량을 계산한다. |

cff1=FC(i,0)*Hz_inv(i,1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:2525` `                  Bio(i,1,iFeO_)=Bio(i,1,iFeO_)+cff1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:113` `# elif defined ECOSIM`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:114` `#  include <ecosim.h>` | C 문서 누락 | ECOSIM은 `cff=dtbio*ABS(Wbio(isink))`로 침강 거리를 계산한다. LINEAR_CONTINUATION, NEUMANN, 기본 경계 재구성을 구분한다. BIO_SEDIMENT는 저층 유출 원소를 대응 무기물로 돌려준다. |
| biology:CF-083 | ecosim — Update the tendency arrays | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:2533` `!  Update the tendency arrays`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:2537` `            itrc=idbio(ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:2540` `                  Bio(i,k,itrc)=Bio(i,k,itrc)+dtbio*Bio_new(i,k,itrc)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:113` `# elif defined ECOSIM`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:114` `#  include <ecosim.h>` | C 문서 누락 | ECOSIM은 Bio_new에 dtbio를 곱해 각 내부 Bio 농도에 더한다. |
| biology:CF-084 | ecosim — Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes to tracer array in time index "nnew". Index "nnew" is solution after advection and mixing and has transport units (m Tunits) hence the increment is multiplied by Hz.  Notice that we need to subtract original values "Bio_old" at the top of the routine to just account for the concentractions affected by BGC processes. This also takes into account any constraints (non-negative concentrations, carbon concentration range) specified before entering BGC kernel. If "Bio" were unchanged by BGC processes, the increment would be exactly zero. Notice that final tracer values, t(:,:,:,nnew,:) are not bounded >=0 so that we can preserve total inventory of nutrients when advection causes tracer concentration to go negative. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:2548` `!  Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:2563` `          itrc=idbio(ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:2566` `               cff=Bio(i,k,itrc)-Bio_old(i,k,itrc)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:2567` `               t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)=t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)+cff*Hz(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:113` `# elif defined ECOSIM`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:114` `#  include <ecosim.h>` | C 문서 누락 | ECOSIM은 Bio와 Bio_old의 차이에 Hz를 곱해 nnew tracer를 갱신한다. 최종 tracer의 비음수 제한은 다시 적용하지 않는다. |

SEDIMENT와 SANDS_PROXY_ISS가 함께 정의되면 부유 퇴적물 농도를 추출한다. |
| biology:CF-088 | ecb — Start internal iterations to achieve convergence of the nonlinear backward-implicit solution. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:671` `! C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되면 6개 추적자에 단조성 제한을 둔 수직 침강을 계산한다. 바닥에 도달한 유기물에는 재부유·매몰·재광물화 처리가 이어진다. 이 ECB 바닥 처리에는 BIO_SEDIMENT 조건이 없다. NET_SULFATE_REDUCTION은 황산염 환원 처리를 선택한다. |
| biology:CF-098 | ecb — Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes to tracer array in time index "nnew". Index "nnew" is solution after advection and mixing and has transport units (m Tunits) hence the increment is multiplied by Hz.  Notice that we need to subtract original values "Bio_old" at the top of the routine to just account for the concentractions affected by BGC processes. This also takes into account any constraints (non-negative concentrations, carbon concentration range) specified before entering BGC kernel. If "Bio" were unchanged by BGC processes, the increment would be exactly zero. Notice that final tracer values, t(:,:,:,nnew,:) are not bounded >=0 so that we can preserve total inventory of N and C even when advection causes tracer concentration to go negative. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1952` `!  Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1969` `            Bio(i,k,iTIC_)=MIN(Bio(i,k,iTIC_), 3000.0_r8)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1970` `            Bio(i,k,iTIC_)=MAX(Bio(i,k,iTIC_), 1.0_r8)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1974` `          ibio=idbio(itrc)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1977` `              cff=Bio(i,k,ibio)-Bio_old(i,k,ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1978` `#ifdef MASKING`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1979` `              cff=cff*rmask(i,j)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1980` `# ifdef WET_DRY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1981` `              cff=cff*rmask_wet(i,j)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1984` `              t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)=t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)+cff*Hz(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되면 생물 농도 변화량을 층 두께와 곱해 수송 추적자에 더한다. TIC에는 1–3000 범위 제한을 둔다. MASKING과 그 안의 WET_DRY 분기가 마스크를 적용한다. |
| biology:CF-099 | ecb — Determine coefficients for surface carbon chemisty.  If land/sea masking, compute only on water points. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2101` `!  Determine coefficients for surface carbon chemisty.  If land/sea`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2106` `# ifdef MASKING`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2107` `        IF (rmask(i,j).gt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2109` `        Tk=T(i)+273.15_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2110` `        centiTk=0.01_r8*Tk`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2111` `        invTk=1.0_r8/Tk`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2112` `        logTk=LOG(Tk)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2113` `        sqrtS=SQRT(S(i))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2114` `        scl=S(i)/1.80655_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2116` `        alk= TAlk(i)*0.000001_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2117` `        dic = TIC(i)*0.000001_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1994` `      SUBROUTINE pCO2_water_RZ (Istr, Iend,                             &` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되고 pCO2_RZ가 정의되면 pCO2_water_RZ를 사용한다. Iteratively solver for computing hydrogen ions [H+] using either:`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2246` `        K12 = K1*K2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2248` `        p5 = -1.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2249` `        p4 = -alk-Kb-K1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2250` `        p3 = dic*K1-alk*(Kb+K1)+Kb*borate+Kw-Kb*K1-K12`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2251` `        p2 = dic*(Kb*K1+2*K12)-alk*(Kb*K1+K12)+Kb*borate*K1             &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2252` `     &       +(Kw*Kb+Kw*K1-Kb*K12)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2253` `        p1 = 2.0_r8*dic*Kb*K12-alk*Kb*K12+Kb*borate*K12                 &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2254` `     &       +Kw*Kb*K1+Kw*K12`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2255` `        p0 = Kw*Kb*K12`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2259` `        pH_guess=pH(i,j)         ! Newton-Raphson`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2260` `        pH_hi=10.0_r8            ! high bracket/bisection`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2261` `        pH_lo=5.0_r8             ! low bracket/bisection`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2265` `        X_guess=10.0_r8**(-pH_guess)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2266` `        X_lo=10.0_r8**(-pH_hi)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2267` `        X_hi=10.0_r8**(-pH_lo)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2268` `        X_mid=0.5_r8*(X_lo+X_hi)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1994` `      SUBROUTINE pCO2_water_RZ (Istr, Iend,                             &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:429` `      integer, parameter :: DoNewton = 0         ! pCO2 solver`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1380` `          CALL pCO2_water_RZ (Istr, Iend, LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,           &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1381` `     &                        IminS, ImaxS, j, DoNewton,                &` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되고 pCO2_RZ가 정의되면 pCO2_water_RZ를 사용한다. 알칼리도 평형의 다항식 계수를 구성한다. 초기 pH 구간은 5–10이다. 호출부의 `DoNewton = 0`에 따라 구간 이분법을 사용한다. [10-14 전수 검증 정정] |
| biology:CF-106 | ecb — Newton-Raphson method. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2271` `!  Newton-Raphson method.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2274` `        IF (DoNewton.eq.1) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2275` `          X=X_guess`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2281` `            fn=((((p5*X+p4)*X+p3)*X+p2)*X+p1)*X+p0`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2287` `            df=(((5*p5*X+4*p4)*X+3*p3)*X+2*p2)*X+p1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2291` `            deltaX=-fn/df`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2295` `            X=X+deltaX`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1994` `      SUBROUTINE pCO2_water_RZ (Istr, Iend,                             &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:429` `      integer, parameter :: DoNewton = 0         ! pCO2 solver`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1380` `          CALL pCO2_water_RZ (Istr, Iend, LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,           &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1381` `     &                        IminS, ImaxS, j, DoNewton,                &` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되고 pCO2_RZ가 정의되면 pCO2_water_RZ를 사용한다. `IF (DoNewton.eq.1) THEN`에 Newton–Raphson 분기가 있다. 그러나 ECB 호출부는 `DoNewton = 0`을 전달하므로 이 분기에 도달하지 않는다. | biology:CF-107 | ecb — Bracket and bisection method. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2299` `!  Bracket and bisection method.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2309` `              IF (Hstep.eq.1) X=X_hi`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2310` `              IF (Hstep.eq.2) X=X_lo`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2311` `              IF (Hstep.eq.3) X=X_mid`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2315` `              fni(Hstep)=((((p5*X+p4)*X+p3)*X+p2)*X+p1)*X+p0`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2320` `            IF (fni(3).eq.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2323` `               ftest=fni(1)/fni(3)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2324` `               IF (ftest.gt.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2325` `                 X_hi=X_mid`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2327` `                 X_lo=X_mid`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2329` `               X_mid=0.5_r8*(X_lo+X_hi)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2335` `          X=X_mid`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1994` `      SUBROUTINE pCO2_water_RZ (Istr, Iend,                             &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:429` `      integer, parameter :: DoNewton = 0         ! pCO2 solver`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1380` `          CALL pCO2_water_RZ (Istr, Iend, LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,           &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1381` `     &                        IminS, ImaxS, j, DoNewton,                &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2079` `      integer, parameter :: IbrackMax = 30`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2307` `          BRACK_IT: DO Ibrack=1,IbrackMax` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되고 pCO2_RZ가 정의되면 pCO2_water_RZ를 사용한다. 호출부의 `DoNewton = 0`은 구간 이분법을 선택한다. 최대 반복 수는 IbrackMax=30이다. `IF (fni(3).eq.0) THEN`이면 반복을 종료한다. | biology:CF-108 | ecb — Determine pCO2. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2339` `!  Determine pCO2.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2344` `        Htotal=X`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2345` `        Htotal2=Htotal*Htotal`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2351` `        CO2star=dic*Htotal2/(Htotal2+K1*Htotal+K1*K2)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2355` `        pH(i,j)=-LOG10(Htotal)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2359` `        pCO2(i)=CO2star*1000000.0_r8/ff`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2361` `# ifdef MASKING`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2363` `        pH(i,j)=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2364` `        pCO2(i)=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1994` `      SUBROUTINE pCO2_water_RZ (Istr, Iend,                             &` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되고 pCO2_RZ가 정의되면 pCO2_water_RZ를 사용한다. 최종 수소 이온 농도로 pH와 `pCO2(i)=CO2star*1000000.0_r8/ff`를 계산한다. MASKING의 육지 분기는 둘을 0으로 둔다. [10-14 전수 검증 정정] |
| biology:CF-109 | ecb — Determine coefficients for surface carbon chemisty.  If land/sea masking, compute only on water points. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2372` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2474` `!  Determine coefficients for surface carbon chemisty.  If land/sea`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2479` `# ifdef MASKING`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2480` `        IF (rmask(i,j).gt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2482` `        Tk=T(i)+273.15_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2483` `        centiTk=0.01_r8*Tk`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2484` `        invTk=1.0_r8/Tk`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2485` `        logTk=LOG(Tk)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2486` `        sqrtS=SQRT(S(i))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2487` `        SO4=19.924_r8*S(i)/(1000.0_r8-1.005_r8*S(i))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2488` `        sqrtSO4=SQRT(SO4)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2489` `        scl=S(i)/1.80655_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2491` `        alk=TAlk(i)*0.000001_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2492` `        dic=TIC(i)*0.000001_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2493` `        phos=PO4b*0.000001_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2494` `        sili=SiO3*0.000001_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2372` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2373` `      SUBROUTINE pCO2_water (Istr, Iend,                                &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1396` `     &                     0.0_r8, 0.0_r8, pH, pCO2)` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되고 pCO2_RZ가 정의되지 않으면 pCO2_water를 사용한다. MASKING이면 물 격자에서 계산한다. 온도·염분 보조 계수와 TIC·알칼리도·인산염·규산염 변환을 수행한다. ECB 호출부는 인산염과 규산염 농도 인수에 각각 `0.0_r8`을 전달한다. [10-14 전수 검증 정정] |

| biology:CF-119 | ecb — Iteratively solver for computing hydrogen ions [H+] using either: | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2372` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2636` `! Newton-Raphson`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2646` `        pH_hi=10.0_r8            ! high bracket/bisection`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2647` `        pH_lo=5.0_r8             ! low bracket/bisection`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2651` `        X_guess=10.0_r8**(-pH_guess)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2652` `        X_lo=10.0_r8**(-pH_hi)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2653` `        X_hi=10.0_r8**(-pH_lo)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2654` `        X_mid=0.5_r8*(X_lo+X_hi)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2372` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2373` `      SUBROUTINE pCO2_water (Istr, Iend,                                &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:429` `      integer, parameter :: DoNewton = 0         ! pCO2 solver`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1389` `          CALL pCO2_water (Istr, Iend, LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,              &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1390` `     &                     IminS, ImaxS, j, DoNewton,                   &` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되고 pCO2_RZ가 정의되지 않으면 pCO2_water를 사용한다. 초기 pH 구간은 5–10이다. 호출부의 `DoNewton = 0`에 따라 알칼리도 평형식을 구간 이분법으로 푼다. [10-14 전수 검증 정정] |

| biology:CF-122 | ecb — Determine pCO2. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:1993` `#ifdef pCO2_RZ`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2372` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2796` `!  Determine pCO2.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2801` `        Htotal=X`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2802` `        Htotal2=Htotal*Htotal`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2808` `        CO2star=dic*Htotal2/(Htotal2+K1*Htotal+K1*K2)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2812` `        pH(i,j)=-LOG10(Htotal)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2816` `        pCO2(i)=CO2star*1000000.0_r8/ff`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2818` `# ifdef MASKING`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2820` `        pH(i,j)=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2821` `        pCO2(i)=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:111` `# elif defined ECB`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:112` `#  include <ecb.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2372` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecb.h:2373` `      SUBROUTINE pCO2_water (Istr, Iend,                                &` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 ECB가 선택되고 pCO2_RZ가 정의되지 않으면 pCO2_water를 사용한다. 최종 수소 이온 농도로 pH와 `pCO2(i)=CO2star*1000000.0_r8/ff`를 계산한다. MASKING의 육지 분기는 둘을 0으로 둔다. [10-14 전수 검증 정정] |

Total biological respiration.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/hypoxia_srm.h:336` `              cff1=dtdays*respiration(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/hypoxia_srm.h:337` `              Bio(i,k,iOxyg)=Bio(i,k,iOxyg)-cff1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/hypoxia_srm.h:338` `              Bio(i,k,iOxyg)=MAX(Bio(i,k,iOxyg),0.0_r8)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:115` `# elif defined HYPOXIA_SRM`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:116` `#  include <hypoxia_srm.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 HYPOXIA_SRM가 선택될 때의 계산이다. `cff1=dtdays*respiration(i,j,k)`를 산소에서 빼고 내부 산소 농도에 0 하한을 둔다. respiration은 별도로 공급되는 3차원 필드이다. |

| biology:CF-130 | hypoxia_srm — Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes to tracer array in time index "nnew". Index "nnew" is solution after advection and mixing and has transport units (m Tunits) hence the increment is multiplied by Hz.  Notice that we need to subtract original values "Bio_old" at the top of the routine to just account for the concentractions affected by BGC processes. This also takes into account any constraints (non-negative concentrations) specified before entering BGC kernel. If "Bio" were unchanged by BGC processes, the increment would be exactly zero. Notice that final tracer values, t(:,:,:,nnew,:) are not bounded >=0 so that we can preserve total inventory of nutrients even when advection causes tracer concentration to go negative. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/hypoxia_srm.h:437` `!  Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/hypoxia_srm.h:452` `          ibio=idbio(itrc)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/hypoxia_srm.h:455` `              cff=Bio(i,k,ibio)-Bio_old(i,k,ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/hypoxia_srm.h:456` `              t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)=t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)+cff*Hz(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:115` `# elif defined HYPOXIA_SRM`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:116` `#  include <hypoxia_srm.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 HYPOXIA_SRM가 선택될 때의 계산이다. `cff=Bio(i,k,ibio)-Bio_old(i,k,ibio)`를 층 두께와 곱해 nnew 추적자에 더한다. 이 최종 갱신에는 0 하한을 적용하지 않는다. |

Stock 8/15/2006`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:429` `              S_fac=SC0+salt*(SC1+salt*(SC2+salt*SC3))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:433` `              G_fac=T_fac*S_fac`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:437` `# ifdef DAILY_SHORTWAVE`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:438` `              Rad=srflx_avg(i,j)*RadScale*EXP(AttW(ng)*z_r(i,j,k))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:439` `# else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:440` `              Rad=srflx(i,j)*RadScale*EXP(AttW(ng)*z_r(i,j,k))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:442` `              IF (z_r(i,j,k).gt.C_depth) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:443` `                cff=Gmax(ng)*G_fac+G_r(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:444` `                G_light=MAX(0.0_r8,cff*TANH(G_eff(ng)*Rad/cff)-G_r(ng))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:446` `                G_light=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:455` `              IF (z_r(i,j,k).gt.C_depth) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:456` `                DIN=DIN_OBS(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:458` `                DIN=DIN_Cdepth(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:464` `              G_DIN=Gmax(ng)*G_fac*DIN/(MAX(Kn(ng),0.0_r8)+DIN)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:469` `              G_rate=MAX(MIN(G_light,G_DIN),0.0_r8)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:470` `              Bio(i,k,iDino)=Bio(i,k,iDino)/(1.0_r8-G_rate*dtdays)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:125` `# elif defined RED_TIDE`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:126` `#  include <red_tide.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 RED_TIDE가 선택될 때의 계산이다. 온도·염분과 광량·질소 제한으로 `G_rate=MAX(MIN(G_light,G_DIN),0.0_r8)`를 구한다. 농도는 `Bio(i,k,iDino)=Bio(i,k,iDino)/(1.0_r8-G_rate*dtdays)`로 갱신한다. DAILY_SHORTWAVE는 광량 공급을 선택한다. |

&`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:579` `     &                 2.0_r8*aL(i,1)-aR(i,1))**2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:588` `                deltaL=MAX(dL(i,k  ),eps)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:589` `                deltaR=MAX(dR(i,k+1),eps)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:590` `                r(i,k)=(deltaR*aR(i,k)+deltaL*aL(i,k+1))/               &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:591` `     &                 (deltaR+deltaL)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:595` `#ifdef NEUMANN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:596` `              r(i,N(ng))=1.5_r8*Bio(i,N(ng),ibio)-0.5_r8*r(i,N(ng)-1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:597` `              r(i,0    )=1.5_r8*Bio(i,1    ,ibio)-0.5_r8*r(i,1      )`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:598` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:599` `              r(i,N(ng))=2.0_r8*Bio(i,N(ng),ibio)-r(i,N(ng)-1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:600` `              r(i,0    )=2.0_r8*Bio(i,1    ,ibio)-r(i,1      )`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:612` `#ifdef LIMIT_INTERIOR`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:613` `                deltaR=r(i,k)-Bio(i,k,ibio)      ! Constrain parabolic`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:614` `                deltaL=Bio(i,k,ibio)-r(i,k-1)    ! segment monotonicity`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:615` `                cffR=2.0_r8*deltaR               ! like in PPM`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:616` `                cffL=2.0_r8*deltaL`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:617` `                IF (deltaR*deltaL.lt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:618` `                  deltaR=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:619` `                  deltaL=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:620` `                ELSE IF (ABS(deltaR).gt.ABS(cffL)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:621` `                  deltaR=cffL`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:622` `                ELSE IF (ABS(deltaL).gt.ABS(cffR)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:623` `                  deltaL=cffR`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:625` `                aR(i,k)=Bio(i,k,ibio)+deltaR`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:626` `                aL(i,k)=Bio(i,k,ibio)-deltaL`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:627` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:628` `                aR(i,k)=r(i,k  )`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:629` `                aL(i,k)=r(i,k-1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:631` `                dL(i,k)=0.5_r8*(aR(i,k)-aL(i,k))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:632` `                dR(i,k)=0.5_r8*(aR(i,k)+aL(i,k))-Bio(i,k,ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:640` `            wdt=-Wbio(iswim)*dtdays`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:643` `                IF (wdt.gt.0.0_r8) THEN           ! downward vertical`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:644` `                  alpha=Hz(i,j,k+1)               ! motion (sinking)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:645` `                  cff =aL(i,k+1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:646` `                  cffL=dL(i,k+1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:647` `                  cffR=dR(i,k+1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:648` `                  dz=wdt`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:650` `                  alpha=-Hz(i,j,k)                ! motion (swimming,`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:651` `                  cff =aR(i,k)                    ! migration)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:652` `                  cffL=-dL(i,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:653` `                  cffR=dR(i,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:654` `                  dz=wdt`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:661` `                alpha=dz/alpha                    ! Courant number`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:662` `                FC(i,k)=dz*(cff+alpha*(cffL-cffR*(3.0_r8-2.0_r8*alpha)))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:666` `              FC(i,0    )=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:667` `              FC(i,N(ng))=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:674` `                cff=(FC(i,k)-FC(i,k-1))*Hz_inv(i,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:675` `                Bio(i,k,ibio)=Bio(i,k,ibio)+cff`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:125` `# elif defined RED_TIDE`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:126` `#  include <red_tide.h>`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:1` `#undef  NEWMAN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:2` `#define LIMIT_INTERIOR` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 RED_TIDE가 선택될 때의 계산이다. 파일에서 LIMIT_INTERIOR를 정의한다. 단조성 제한과 계면 재구성 뒤 `wdt=-Wbio(iswim)*dtdays`의 부호로 침강·유영 방향을 선택한다. 위·아래 경계 플럭스는 0이다. 파일의 `#undef  NEWMAN`은 NEUMANN을 해제하지 않는다. |
| biology:CF-138 | red_tide — Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes to tracer array in time index "nnew". Index "nnew" is solution after advection and mixing and has transport units (m Tunits) hence the increment is multiplied by Hz.  Notice that we need to subtract original values "Bio_old" at the top of the routine to just account for the concentrations affected by BGC processes. This also takes into account any constraints (non-negative concentrations, carbon concentration range) specified before entering BGC kernel. If "Bio" were unchanged by BGC processes, the increment would be exactly zero. Notice that final tracer values, t(:,:,:,nnew,:) are not bounded >=0 so that we can preserve total inventory of nutrients when advection causes tracer concentration to go negative. | 대상 문서에 대응 서술이 없다. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:683` `!  Update global tracer variables: Add increment due to BGC processes`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:698` `          ibio=idbio(itrc)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:701` `              cff=Bio(i,k,ibio)-Bio_old(i,k,ibio)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/red_tide.h:702` `              t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)=t(i,j,k,nnew,ibio)+cff*Hz(i,j,k)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:2` `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:125` `# elif defined RED_TIDE`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/biology.F:126` `#  include <red_tide.h>` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·BIOLOGY 조건에서 RED_TIDE가 선택될 때의 계산이다. `cff=Bio(i,k,ibio)-Bio_old(i,k,ibio)`를 층 두께와 곱해 nnew 추적자에 더한다. 이 최종 갱신에는 0 하한을 적용하지 않는다. |

| biology:NT-002 | Fennel 2006 서술의 확장 판 | `models/ROMS/source-analysis/roms_biology.md:51` `Fennel et al. 2006 의 carbon/nitrogen/oxygen 모델:`<br>`models/ROMS/source-analysis/roms_biology.md:42` `\| **Fennel** (BIO_FENNEL) \| fennel.h(2375) \| C-N-O 완전 biogeochem (§3) \|` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:10` `!  This routine computes the  biological sources and sinks for the     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:11` `!  Fennel et al. (2006) ecosystem model. Then, it adds those terms     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:12` `!  to the global biological fields.                                    !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:13` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:14` `!  This model is loosely based on the model by Fasham et al. (1990)    !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:15` `!  but it differs in many respects.  The detailed equations of the     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:16` `!  nitrogen cycling component  are given in  Fennel et al. (2006).     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:17` `!  Nitrogen is the  fundamental elemental  currency in this model.     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:18` `!  This model was adapted from a code written originally  by  John     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:19` `!  Moisan and Emanule DiLorenzo.                                       !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:20` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:21` `!  It is recommended to activate always the  "BIO_SEDIMENT" option     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:22` `!  to ensure conservation of mass by converting the organic matter     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:23` `!  that is sinking out of the bottom most grid cell into inorganic     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:24` `!  nutrients (i.e.,  instantanaous remineralization  at the water-     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:25` `!  sediment interface). Additionally, the "DENITRIFICATION" option     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:26` `!  can be activated.  Hence, a fraction of the instantenous bottom     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:27` `!  remineralization is  assumed to  occur  through  the  anearobic     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:28` `!  (denitrification)  pathway  and  thus  lost  from the  pool  of     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:29` `!  biologically availalbe fixed nitrogen. See Fennel et al. (2006)     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:30` `!  for details.                                                        !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:31` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:32` `!  Additional  options can be  activated to  enable  simulation of     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:33` `!  inorganic carbon and dissolved oxygen.  Accounting of inorganic     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:34` `!  carbon is activated by the "CARBON" option,  and results in two     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:35` `!  additional  biological  tracer  variables:  DIC and alkalinity.     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:36` `!  See Fennel et al. (2008) for details.                               !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:37` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:38` `!  If the "pCO2_RZ" options is activated, in addition to "CARBON",     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:39` `!  the carbonate system  routines by Zeebe and Wolf-Gladrow (2001)     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:40` `!  are used,  while the  OCMIP  standard routines are the default.     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:41` `!  There are two different ways of treating alkalinity.  It can be     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:42` `!  treated diagnostically (default),  in this case alkalinity acts     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:43` `!  like a passive tracer  that is  not affected  by changes in the     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:44` `!  concentration of  nitrate or ammonium.  However,  if the option     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:45` `!  "TALK_NONCONSERV" is used,  the alkalinity  will be affected by     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:46` `!  sources and sinks in nitrate. See Fennel et al. (2008) for more     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:47` `!  details.                                                            !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:48` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:49` `!  If the "OXYGEN" option is activated,  one additional biological     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:50` `!  tracer variable for dissolved oxygen. "OXYGEN" can be activated     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:51` `!  independently of the  "CARBON"  option. If "OCMIP_OXYGEN_SC" is     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:52` `!  used, in addition to "OXYGEN",  the Schmidt number of oxygen in     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:53` `!  seawater will be  computed  using the  formulation  proposed by     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:54` `!  Keeling et al. (1998, Global Biogeochem. Cycles,  12, 141-163).     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:55` `!  Otherwise,  the Wanninkhof (1992)  formula will be used. See        !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:56` `!  Fennel et al. (2013) for more details.                              !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:57` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:58` `!***********************************************************************`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:59` `!  UPDATE Sept 2020                                                    !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:60` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:61` `!  In this  version  additional  tracers,  additional   alkalinity     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:62` `!  fluxes, and an updated parameterization of air-sea O2  and  CO2     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:63` `!  fluxes are added as described in Laurent et al. (2017).             !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:64` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:65` `!  If "PO4" is activated,   one  additional biological tracer   is     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:66` `!  added  representing phosphate.  With this option  phytoplankton     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:67` `!  growth can be limited by either  nitrogen and phosphorus.  This     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:68` `!  option was introduced in Laurent et al. (2012).                     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:69` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:70` `!  If "RIVER_DON"  is activated,  an additional  biological tracer     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:71` `!  (or 2 if "CARBON" is defined) is added representing non-sinking     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:72` `!  dissolved organic matter from rivers as  described in Yu et al.     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:73` `!  (2015).                                                             !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:74` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:75` `!  If the "RW14_OXYGEN_SC" and/or  "RW14_CO2_SC" options are used,     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:76` `!  the model will use Wanninkhof (2014) air-sea flux parameteri-       !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:77` `!  zation.   With the  "TALK_NONCONSERV"  option,   alkalinity  is     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:78` `!  affected by biological fluxes   as described in  Laurent et al.     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:79` `!  (2017).                                                             !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:80` `!                                                                      !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:81` `!  With the  "PCO2AIR_DATA"  option,   atmospheric pCO2  uses  the     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:82` `!  climatology of  Laurent et al. (2017).   The  "PCO2AIR_SECULAR"     !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:184` `#ifdef CARBON`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:185` `# ifdef OXYGEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:186` `#  if defined PO4 && defined RIVER_DON`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:187` `      NBT=15`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:188` `#  elif defined RIVER_DON && !defined PO4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:189` `      NBT=14`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:190` `#  elif defined PO4 && !defined RIVER_DON`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:191` `      NBT=13`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:192` `#  else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:193` `      NBT=12`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:194` `#  endif`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:195` `# else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:196` `#  if defined PO4 && defined RIVER_DON`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:197` `      NBT=14`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:198` `#  elif defined RIVER_DON && !defined PO4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:199` ` | biology:NT-003 | CARBON 추가 추적자 | `models/ROMS/source-analysis/roms_biology.md:53` `` - **CARBON** option: + `TIC`(DIC 용존무기탄소) + `TAlk`(총 알칼리도) + large detritus C. 탄산계 `Zeebe & Wolf-Gladrow 2001`(또는 `pCO2_RZ`). `` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:513` `# ifdef CARBON`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:514` `      iLDeC=ic+1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:515` `      iSDeC=ic+2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:516` `      iTIC_=ic+3`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:517` `      iTAlk=ic+4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:518` `      ic=ic+4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:519` `#  ifdef RIVER_DON`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:520` `      iRDeC=ic+1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:521` `      ic=ic+1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel_mod.h:522` `#  endif` | C 문서 누락 | CARBON은 iLDeC, iSDeC, iTIC_, iTAlk를 추가한다. CARBON과 RIVER_DON을 함께 켜면 iRDeC도 추가한다. |

| biology:NT-007 | NPZD의 공통 과정과 서로 다른 식 | `models/ROMS/source-analysis/roms_biology.md:60` `` 4 상태변수 `N`(nutrient)-`P`(phytoplankton)-`Z`(zooplankton)-`D`(detritus). 광합성 uptake(Michaelis-Menten N 제한 × light) → grazing → mortality → remineralization → N 복귀. Franks et al. 1986 / Powell et al. 2006. iron variant = + 철 제한(HNLC 해역). `` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:316` `          cff1=dtdays*Vm_NO3(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:317` `          DO k=1,N(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:318` `            DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:319` `              cff=Bio(i,k,iPhyt)*                                       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:320` `     &            cff1*EXP(K_ext(ng)*z_r(i,j,k))/                       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:321` `     &            (K_NO3(ng)+Bio(i,k,iNO3_))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:322` `              Bio(i,k,iNO3_)=Bio(i,k,iNO3_)/                            &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:323` `     &                       (1.0_r8+cff)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:324` `              Bio(i,k,iPhyt)=Bio(i,k,iPhyt)+                            &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:325` `     &                       Bio(i,k,iNO3_)*cff`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:326` `            END DO`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:327` `          END DO`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:328` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:329` `! (rate: PhyMR).`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:331` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:332` `          cff1=dtdays*ZooGR(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:333` `          cff2=dtdays*PhyMR(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:334` `          cff3=K_phy(ng)*K_phy(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:335` `          DO k=1,N(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:336` `            DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Franks.h:337` `              cff=Bio(i,k,iZoop)*Bio(i,k,iPhyt)*cff1/ cff1=dtdays*Vm_NO3(ng)*PhyIS(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:372` `          cff2=Vm_NO3(ng)*Vm_NO3(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:373` `          cff3=PhyIS(ng)*PhyIS(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:374` `          DO k=1,N(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:375` `            DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:376` `              cff4=1.0_r8/SQRT(cff2+cff3*Light(i,k)*Light(i,k))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:377` `              cff=Bio(i,k,iPhyt)*                                       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:378` `     &            cff1*cff4*Light(i,k)/                                 &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:379` `     &            (K_NO3(ng)+Bio(i,k,iNO3_))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:380` ` &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:382` `     &                       Bio(i,k,iNO3_)*cff`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:383` `            END DO`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:384` `          END DO`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:385` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:386` `!  Grazing on phytoplankton by zooplankton (ZooGR rate) using the Ivlev`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:387` `!  formulation (Ivlev, 1955) and lost of phytoplankton to the nitrate`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:388` `!  pool as function of "sloppy feeding" and metabolic processes`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:389` `!  (ZooEEN and ZooEED fractions).`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:390` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:391` `          cff1=dtdays*ZooGR(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:392` `          cff2=1.0_r8-ZooEEN(ng)-ZooEED(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:393` `          DO k=1,N(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:394` `            DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:395` `              cff=Bio(i,k,iZoop)*                                       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:396` `     &            cff1*(1.0_r8-EXP(-Ivlev(ng)*Bio(i,k,iPhyt)))/         &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell.h:397` `     &            Bio(i,k,iPhyt)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:443` `#ifdef IRON_LIMIT`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:444` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:445` `!  Calculate growth reduction factor due to iron limitation.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:446` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:447` `              FNratio=Bio(i,k,iFphy)/MAX(MinVal,Bio(i,k,iPhyt))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:448` `              FCratio=FNratio*FeN2FeC`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:449` `              FCratioE=B_Fe(ng)*Bio(i,k,iFdis)**A_Fe(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:450` `              Flimit=FCratio*FCratio/                                   &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:451` `     &               (FCratio*FCratio+K_FeC(ng)*K_FeC(ng))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:452` `!`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:453` `              Nlimit=1.0_r8/(K_NO3(ng)+Bio(i,k,iNO3_))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:454` `              FNlim=MIN(1.0_r8,Flimit/(Bio(i,k,iNO3_)*Nlimit))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:455` `#endif`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:456` `              cff4=1.0_r8/SQRT(cff2+cff3*Light(i,k)*Light(i,k))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:457` `              cff=Bio(i,k,iPhyt)*                                       &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:458` `#ifdef IRON_LIMIT`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:459` `     &            cff1*cff4*Light(i,k)*FNlim*Nlimit`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:460` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:461` `     &            cff1*cff4*Light(i,k)/                                 &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron.h:462` `     &            (K_NO3(ng)+Bio(i,k,iNO3_))` | C 문서 누락 | Franks는 EXP(K_ext*z_r)를 광 제한에 쓴다. Powell은 PAR와 self-shading을 계산한다. Franks·Powell·iron의 K_NO3는 현재 식에서 농도와 더해진다. Fennel K_NO3는 농도에 곱해지는 역수 계수다. |

This has been removed and may`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:555` `!        result in differences in deeper waters.`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:562` `              FV1=MAX(1.0_r8,(Bio(i,k,iPhyC(iphy))/refuge(i,k,iphy)))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim.h:563` `              graz_act(i,k,iphy)=HsGRZ(iphy,ng)*LOG(FV1)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim_inp.h:74` `            CASE ('NFIX_flag')`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim_inp.h:75` `              Npts=load_l(Nval, Cval, Ngrids, NFIX_flag)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim_inp.h:180` `            CASE ('RefugeDep')`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/ecosim_inp.h:181` `              Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, Nphy, Ngrids, RefugeDep)` | C 문서 누락 | 입력 예의 수치와 생략 시 자동 초기화를 구분한다. main3d의 생물 호출에는 BIOLOGY 조건이 있으나 Lbiology 런타임 가드는 없다. Fennel 입력의 `CASE ('Vadvection')` 중복은 ADJOINT·TANGENT·TL_IOMS 중 하나가 정의될 때 드러난다. Fennel·ECB의 PhyIP와 EcoSim NFIX_flag·RefugeDep는 입력·출력에 있으나 해당 비선형 반응식에는 소비되지 않는다. EcoSim 주석은 깊이별 refuge 처리를 제거했다고 명시한다. 단위 문제는 UN-012·UN-015의 입력 설명 오기 B와 UN-013·UN-014·UN-016의 메타데이터 결함 후보 E로 구분한다. [10-14 전수 검증 정정] |

| biology:WK-029 | npzd_Powell 계산 스위치의 모델 이름 | `npzd_Powell.in.wiki:52` <code>*This switch to control the computation of npzd_Franks within nested and/or multiple connected grids. By default this switch is set to TRUE in &lt;span class="red"&gt;mod_scalars.F&lt;/span&gt; for all grids. &#91;&#91;Variables#Ngrids\|Ngrids&#93;&#93; values are expected. The '''user''' has the option, for example, to compute the biology in just one of the nested grids. If so, this switch needs to be consistent with the dimension parameter &#91;&#91;Variables#NBT\|NBT&#93;&#93; in &lt;span class="red"&gt;mod_param.F&lt;/span&gt;. In order to make the model more efficient in memory usage, &#91;&#91;Variables#NBT\|NBT(:)&#93;&#93; should be zero in such grids.</code> | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell_inp.h:10` `!  This routine reads in Powell et al. (2006) ecosystem model input    !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_Powell_inp.h:11` `!  parameters. They are specified in input script "npzd_Powell.in".    !` | B 문서 오기 | Lbiology 설명의 모델 이름을 npzd_Powell로 고친다. |
| biology:WK-030 | npzd_iron 계산 스위치의 모델 이름 | `npzd_iron.in.wiki:64` <code>*This switch to control the computation of npzd_Franks within nested and/or multiple connected grids. By default this switch is set to TRUE in &lt;span class="red"&gt;mod_scalars.F&lt;/span&gt; for all grids. &#91;&#91;Variables#Ngrids\|Ngrids&#93;&#93; values are expected. The '''user''' has the option, for example, to compute the biology in just one of the nested grids. If so, this switch needs to be consistent with the dimension parameter &#91;&#91;Variables#NBT\|NBT&#93;&#93; in &lt;span class="red"&gt;mod_param.F&lt;/span&gt;. In order to make the model more efficient in memory usage, &#91;&#91;Variables#NBT\|NBT(:)&#93;&#93; should be zero in such grids.</code> | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron_inp.h:10` `!  This routine reads in NPZD iron (Fiechter, et al. 2009) ecosystem   !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/npzd_iron_inp.h:11` `!  model input parameters. They are specified in input script          !` | B 문서 오기 | Lbiology 설명의 모델 이름을 npzd_iron로 고친다. |

Pigs_XY의 X는 식물플랑크톤 그룹이고 Y는 색소 번호다. standard_name은 색소 이름 뒤에 식물플랑크톤 이름을 붙인다. 따라서 위키 일반식의 `<X>_pigment_by_<Y>`는 두 자리의 역할을 뒤바꿨다. 생성 조건은 `IF (PIG(PHY(i),j).gt.0) THEN`이다. [10-14 전수 검증 정정] |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-SN302 | river_alkalinity standard_name | `varinfo.yaml.wiki:1109` `\| <span class="mediumOrchid">river_alkalinity</span>`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:1110` `\| <span class="twilightBlue">alkalinity_transport_expressed_as_mole_equivalent_into_sea_water_from_rivers</span>` | `d2530aaa:ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:2069` `  - variable:       river_alkalinity                                 # Input`<br>`d2530aaa:ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:2070` `    standard_name:  alkalinity_transport_expressed_as_mole_equivalent_into_sea_water_from_rivers`<br>`98adef8a^:ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:2069` `  - variable:       river_alkalinity                                 # Input`<br>`98adef8a^:ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:2070` `    standard_name:  alkalinity_transport_expressed_as_mole_equivalent_into_sea_water_from_rivers`<br>`ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:2941` `  - variable:       river_TAlk                                       # Input`<br>`ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:2942` `    standard_name:  alkalinity_transport_expressed_as_mole_equivalent_into_sea_water_from_rivers`<br>git log: `d2530aaa 2022-02-27 src:trac:910 https://www.myroms.org/projects/src/ticket/910`<br>git log: `98adef8a 2023-05-17 src:trac:936 https://www.myroms.org/projects/src/ticket/936` | A 판 차이 | 과거 변수 이름은 river_alkalinity이다. 현재 변수 이름은 river_TAlk이다. 변수 이름의 변경 커밋은 `98adef8a`(2023-05-17)이다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-Cini_fennel-V027 | ini_fennel.cdl 변수 NO3 | Data/ROMS/CDL/ini_fennel.cdl:124 `	float NO3(ocean_time, s_rho, eta_rho, xi_rho) ;`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/ini_fennel.cdl:125 `		NO3:long_name = "nitrate concentration" ;`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/ini_fennel.cdl:126 `		NO3:units = "millimole_N03 meter-3" ;`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/ini_fennel.cdl:127 `		NO3:time = "ocean_time" ;`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/ini_fennel.cdl:128 `		NO3:coordinates = "lon_rho lat_rho s_rho ocean_time" ;` | `ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:4194` `  - variable:       NO3                                              # Input/Output`<br>`ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:4200` `    index_code:     idTvar(iNO3_)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_state_nlm_nf90.h:756` `     &                          TRIM(Vname(1,idTvar(itrc))), varid)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_state_nlm_nf90.h:505` `#ifdef SOLVE3D` | B 문서 오기 | 이름·rho 격자·단위의 물리 크기는 대응한다. units의 N03은 NO3의 숫자 0 오기다. 질소 몰농도 표기를 사용하는 현재 YAML과 함께 정리해야 한다. |
| sediment-ice-vegetation:RBO1-001 | behavior_oyster.in 역할과 FBIONAM | `behavior_oyster.in.wiki:3` <code>The &lt;span class="red"&gt;behavior_oyster.in&lt;/span&gt; file provides the initial locations of the float trajectories to compute. This standard input ASCII file is organized in several sections as shown below, with links to more detailed explanation where required.</code> | `ROMS/Utility/read_fltpar.F:85` `            CASE ('FBIONAM')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_fltpar.F:89` `              fbionam=TRIM(ADJUSTL(Cval(Nval)))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:64` `            CASE ('Larvae_size0')`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:65` `              Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, Ngrids, Larvae_size0)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:70` `            CASE ('food_supply')`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:71` `              Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, Ngrids, food_supply)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:124` `            CASE ('swim_table')`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:130` `              READ (inp,*,ERR=20,END=30)                                &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:131` `                   ((swim_table(i,j),i=1,swim_Im),j=1,swim_Jm)` | B 문서 오기 | behavior_oyster.in은 유생 성장·행동 매개변수와 조회표를 제공한다. |

| sediment-ice-vegetation:RBO1-008 | DS 정의와 단위 | `behavior_oyster.in.wiki:22` `'''DS:'''     salinity change rate (1/s)`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:86` `!   DS: salinity change rate (1/s)`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:275` `!  DS:     salinity change rate (1/s)` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:249` `          dsalt=track(ifTvar(isalt),nfp1,l)-                            &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:250` `     &          track(ifTvar(isalt),nf  ,l)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:352` `          IF (ABS(dsalt).lt.0.00001_r8) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:353` `            dsalt=0.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:355` `          IF (dsalt.gt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:356` `            SwimTimeNew=MIN(SwimTime+dsalt*slope_Sinc(ng),swim_Tmax(ng))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:358` `            SwimTimeNew=MAX(SwimTime+dsalt*slope_Sdec(ng),swim_Tmin(ng))` | B 문서 오기 | 현재 굴 유생 코드의 DS 입력은 염분 변화량이며 시간으로 나눈 변화율이 아니다. |
| sediment-ice-vegetation:RBO1-015 | swim 축 주석의 대상 | `behavior_oyster.in.wiki:60` `starting value for temperature I-axis`<br>`behavior_oyster.in.wiki:60` `starting value for larval size J-axis`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:111` `       swim_L0 =  55.0d0    ! starting value for temperature I-axis`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:112` `       swim_T0 =  12.0d0    ! starting value for larval size J-axis`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:113` `       swim_DL =  5.0d0     ! larval size J-axis increment`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:114` `       swim_DT =  1.0d0     ! temperature I-axis increment`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:349` `!   swim_L0        Starting value for larval size (um) I-axis`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:351` `!   swim_T0        Starting value for temperature (Celsius) J-axis`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:353` `!   swim_DL        Larval size (um) I-axis increment`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:355` `!   swim_DT        Temperature (Celsius) J-axis increment` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:370` `            my_size=MIN(MAX(swim_L0,Lsize),                             &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:371` `     &                  swim_L0+swim_DL*REAL(swim_Im-1,r8))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:372` `            my_temp=MIN(MAX(swim_T0,temp),                              &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:373` `     &                  swim_T0+swim_DT*REAL(swim_Jm-1,r8))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:375` `            i1=INT(1.0_r8+(my_size-swim_L0)*oswim_DL)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:377` `            j1=INT(1.0_r8+(my_temp-swim_T0)*oswim_DT)` | B 문서 오기 | 유영 조회표의 I축은 유생 크기이며 J축은 수온이다. |
| sediment-ice-vegetation:RBO1-016 | slope_Sdec 계수 기호 | `behavior_oyster.in.wiki:48` <code>&#91;&#91;Variables#slope_Sdec\|slope_Sdec&#93;&#93; == 0.0668d0  ! coefficient {d}</code><br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:93` `    slope_Sdec == 0.0668d0  ! coefficient {d} due to decreasing salinity`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:328` `!   slope_Sdec     Coefficient {e} due to decreasing salinity` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:98` `            CASE ('slope_Sdec')`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:99` `              Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, Ngrids, slope_Sdec)`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:80` `! where     TS =  c * DS + d,    for increasing salinity gradient DS`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:81` `!           TS = -e * DS + f,    for decreasing salinity gradient DS` | B 문서 오기 | 문서의 감소 염분식에서 slope_Sdec에 붙일 매개변수 기호는 e이다. 실제 코드와 문서식의 차이는 RBO1-010에서 구분한다. |
| sediment-ice-vegetation:RBO1-017 | Grate_DL 단위 | `User/External/behavior_oyster.in:394` `!   Grate_DL       Larval size (mg) J-axis increment` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:271` `          my_size=MIN(MAX(Grate_L0,Lsize),                              &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:272` `     &                Grate_L0+Grate_DL*REAL(Grate_Jm-1,r8))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:276` `          j1=INT(1.0_r8+(my_size-Grate_L0)*oGrate_DL)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:280` `          q2=(my_size-(Grate_L0+REAL(j1-1,r8)*Grate_DL))*oGrate_DL` | B 문서 오기 | Grate_DL의 길이축 간격 단위는 um이다. |

number of values in food supply I-axis`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:186` `      Grate_Jm =  52        ! number of values in larval size J-axis`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:192` `   Grate_table =  larval growth rate look table, Grate(31,52)` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:126` `                allocate ( swim_table(swim_Im,swim_Jm) )`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:130` `              READ (inp,*,ERR=20,END=30)                                &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:131` `                   ((swim_table(i,j),i=1,swim_Im),j=1,swim_Jm)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:152` `                allocate ( Gfactor_table(Gfactor_Im,Gfactor_Jm) )`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:156` `              READ (inp,*,ERR=20,END=30)                                &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:157` `                   ((Gfactor_table(i,j),i=1,Gfactor_Im),j=1,Gfactor_Jm)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:178` `                allocate ( Grate_table(Grate_Im,Grate_Jm) )`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:182` `              READ (inp,*,ERR=20,END=30)                                &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:183` `                   ((Grate_table(i,j),i=1,Grate_Im),j=1,Grate_Jm)` | B 문서 오기 | 조회표 Im/Jm은 해당 수치표를 읽기 전에 설정해야 한다. |
| sediment-ice-vegetation:RBO1-003 | 성장 기본식 | `behavior_oyster.in.wiki:18` `d(Lsize)/d(t) = growth(food,Lsize) * Gfactor(T,S) * turb_ef`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:255` `!    d(Lsize)/d(t) = growth(food,Lsize) * Gfactor(T,S) * turb_ef` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:325` `          IF (Lsize.gt.turb_size(ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:326` `            IF (Lturb.gt.turb_crit(ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:327` `              turb_ef=turb_base(ng)*                                    &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:328` `     &                EXP(-turb_rate(ng)*(Lturb-turb_mean(ng)))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:330` `              turb_ef=turb_slop(ng)*Lturb+turb_axis(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:332` `            track(ibrhs,nfp1,l)=Grate*Gfactor*turb_ef*sec2day`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:333` `          ELSE`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:334` `            track(ibrhs,nfp1,l)=Larvae_GR0(ng)*Gfactor*sec2day` | C 문서 누락 | `IF (Lsize.gt.turb_size(ng)) THEN`에서는 `track(ibrhs,nfp1,l)=Grate*Gfactor*turb_ef*sec2day`를 사용한다. ELSE에서는 `track(ibrhs,nfp1,l)=Larvae_GR0(ng)*Gfactor*sec2day`를 사용한다. 작은 유생 경로는 먹이 조회율과 탁도 인자를 사용하지 않는다. 문서는 이 조건을 적지 않는다. [10-14 전수 검증 정정] |
| sediment-ice-vegetation:RBO1-006 | 탁도 임계값의 등호 | `behavior_oyster.in.wiki:22` `for turbidity < 0.1 g/l`<br>`behavior_oyster.in.wiki:22` `for turbidity > 0.1 g/l` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:326` `            IF (Lturb.gt.turb_crit(ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:329` `            ELSE`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:330` `              turb_ef=turb_slop(ng)*Lturb+turb_axis(ng)`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:64` `     turb_crit == 0.1d0     ! critical turbidity value (g/l)` | C 문서 누락 | 굴 유생의 탁도 보정에서 `IF (Lturb.gt.turb_crit(ng)) THEN`은 지수 분기이다. Lturb와 입력 임계값이 같으면 ELSE의 선형 분기를 쓴다. |
| sediment-ice-vegetation:RCO1-036 | BIO_SEDIMENT의 동반 생지화학 옵션 | 해당 문서에 서술 없음. | `ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1473` `            cff2=4.0_r8/16.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1474` `# ifdef OXYGEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1475` `            cff3=115.0_r8/16.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1476` `            cff4=106.0_r8/16.0_r8`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1483` `# ifdef DENITRIFICATION`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1484` `                Bio(i,1,iNH4_)=Bio(i,1,iNH4_)+cff1*cff2`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1492` `#  ifdef PO4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1493` `                Bio(i,1,iPO4_)=Bio(i,1,iPO4_)+cff1*R_P2N(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1495` `#  ifdef OXYGEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1496` `                Bio(i,1,iOxyg)=Bio(i,1,iOxyg)-cff1*cff3`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1499` `                Bio(i,1,iNH4_)=Bio(i,1,iNH4_)+cff1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1500` `#   ifdef PO4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1501` `                Bio(i,1,iPO4_)=Bio(i,1,iPO4_)+cff1*R_P2N(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1503` `#  ifdef OXYGEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1504` `                Bio(i,1,iOxyg)=Bio(i,1,iOxyg)-cff1*cff4`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1506` `#  if defined CARBON && defined TALK_NONCONSERV`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1507` `                Bio(i,1,iTAlk)=Bio(i,1,iTAlk)+cff1`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1464` `#ifdef BIO_SEDIMENT`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1498` `# else`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1502` `#   endif`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1505` `#  endif`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1508` `#  endif`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/fennel.h:1509` `# endif` | C 문서 누락 | Fennel의 BIO_SEDIMENT에서 DENITRIFICATION은 NH4 복귀량에 4/16을 곱한다. OXYGEN은 해당 분기의 산소 소모 계수를 사용한다. PO4는 인산염 복귀를 추가한다. CARBON과 TALK_NONCONSERV의 인용한 질소 재광물화 알칼리도 복귀는 비 DENITRIFICATION 분기에 속한다. |

| sediment-ice-vegetation:RBO1-009 | 증가 염분의 TS 식 | `behavior_oyster.in.wiki:22` `TS =  c * DS + d`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:80` `! where     TS =  c * DS + d,    for increasing salinity gradient DS`<br>`User/External/behavior_oyster.in:265` `! TS =  c * DS + d,    for increasing salinity gradient DS        !`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:221` `              track(iswim,i,l)=0.5_r8*(swim_Tmin(ng)+swim_Tmax(ng))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:249` `          dsalt=track(ifTvar(isalt),nfp1,l)-                            &`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:250` `     &          track(ifTvar(isalt),nf  ,l)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:355` `          IF (dsalt.gt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats.h:356` `            SwimTimeNew=MIN(SwimTime+dsalt*slope_Sinc(ng),swim_Tmax(ng))`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:92` `            CASE ('swim_Sinc')`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/Biology/oyster_floats_inp.h:93` `              Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, Ngrids, swim_Sinc)` | E 코드 결함 후보 (중간) | 염분 증가 식은 이전 SwimTime에 변화를 누적한다. 문서의 절편식과 다른 후보를 E로 유지한다. 근거 강도는 중간이다. |
